Generated from catalog/devices/*.yaml by :address-catalog:compileCatalog. Do not edit. Property-level evidence is queryable through the same compiled model.

writeWidths is the base declaration for unbound and other profiles. Optional writeWidthsByProfile selects an exact list only from the actual owner-bound component profile; queries report componentProfile, declaredWriteWidths and effectiveWriteWidths separately. Profile permissions remain subject to component state, lifecycle and whole-access validation. No ready, IRQ or DMA behavior is implied.

## Logical protocol: rtc-power-register

Component rtc, port rtc.registers. Selector unit; 7 selector bits / 16 word bits, wire order big. No CPU physical base or device read is implied. Access is selected only by the actual owner profile; stateful/transaction checks remain in Kotlin. Initial/reset values are UNKNOWN.

| Register ID | Selector | Profile read / write / status | Fields | Evidence |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| reg.rtc.stage-low | 0x1d | disabled: false / false / DISABLED; gfx100s-rtc-functional-experimental-v1: false / true / IMPLEMENTED_FUNCTIONAL_HYPOTHESIS; gfx100s-rtc-scripted-stage-read-experimental-v2: true / true / EXPERIMENTAL_INPUT | reg.rtc.stage-low.raw[0:16] | [source](../../evidence/refactor/rtc29-stop-research-new-01/qualification-06.generated.json); [source](../../components/rtc/src/main/kotlin/com/github/nyabanban/fuji/emulator/components/rtc/RtcBank.kt) |
